# KTM 125 XC 2023: aftermarket parts

4641 listings whose stated fitment names this bike: 430 researched parts (with what they change and rider findings), 782 dealer and retailer listings specific to this bike, and 3429 universal retailer listings (oil, grips, batteries and the like, counted below; every listing is in [parts.csv](../parts.csv)). Research candidates: check fitment on the maker's page before relying on a row. Back to [the family catalog](../CATALOG.md).
